- **flow-epic-create**: Supervisor skill for the epic-designer phase. Drives one epic end-to-end (clarify → design → validate → commit → open design PR → review checkpoint → approve/redirect/cancel) inside a single Claude Code session spawned by `flow epic create "&lt;prompt&gt;"`. Use ONLY when invoked by that seed prompt or via an explicit `/flow-epic-create`. It opens a reviewable design PR and STOPS at the checkpoint — it never launches a feature `flow feature create`, computes a DAG frontier, or merges the design PR. One long-running supervisor turn per phase, not a sub-agent. ([gavingolden/flow](https://github.com/gavingolden/flow/tree/HEAD/skills/pipeline/flow-epic-create) · ★1)
- **flow-epic-run**: Playbook skill for driving an epic, one reconciled step at a time.
